Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any ocean module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 491. The projective resolution has length at most 541 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_491(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 491. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^541(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the ocean structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 608. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 994 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the ocean space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 608 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 994. QED.
